## Best date plans

### 2026-10-24 — Fall Harvest Festival + Agi's Counter

**Fall Harvest Festival, then a meal.** Eastern European cafe at Agi's Counter. It is a 6-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=990+Washington+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=818+Franklin+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.bbg.org/visit/calendar) · [Restaurant](https://www.agiscounter.com/)

### 2026-10-18 — Smorgasburg Diwali: Festival of Bites at Prospect Park + Rucola

**Smorgasburg Diwali: Festival of Bites at Prospect Park, then a meal.** Italian at Rucola. It is 2.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Breeze+Hill%2C+Prospect+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=190+Dean+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/smorgasburg-diwali-festival-of-bites/) · [Event booking](https://smorgasburg.com/ny-events/diwali-pp) · [Restaurant](https://www.rucolabrooklyn.com/)

### 2026-10-24 — Smorgasburg Diwali at Williamsburg + Laser Wolf Brooklyn

**Smorgasburg Diwali at Williamsburg, then a meal.** Israeli grill at Laser Wolf Brooklyn. It is a 4-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=90+Kent+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&destination=97+Wythe+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://smorgasburg.com/ny-events/diwali-wb) · [Restaurant](https://www.laserwolfbrooklyn.com/)

**Dinner, then NYC in Transit: Carlito's Way in 35mm.** Korean at Somssi. It is a 4-minute walk from the venue. NYC in Transit: Carlito's Way in 35mm starts at 7:50 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=60+MacDougal+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&destination=209+W+Houston+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://filmforum.org/series/nyc-in-transit) · [Restaurant](https://www.somssinyc.com/)

**Timing:** Runs during the Sunday Prospect Park Smorgasburg market hours.
**Practical note:** Outdoor market; NWS forecast is sunny and cool, good for walking and grazing.
**Why now:** This is the Prospect Park date of Smorgasburg's 2026 Diwali special event.

## Where to eat

Verified against captured public evidence and limited follow-up official pages as of 2026-10-18; kept sourced current restaurants, specific timed offers, tasting menus, and dated events, while rejecting duplicate cards and unsupported ordinary-menu duplicates.

No Michelin-star claims were kept. Kirbee’s source mentions Michelin-related recognition for Texas pitmaster Chuck Charnichart/Barbs B Q, not a Michelin star for Kirbee’s. From the C as in Charlie/Kisa team, this is a useful affordable sit-down dinner option with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rotating specials, salad bar, and unlimited buffet trips.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 ages 65+; $13.99 kids ages 4-9; desserts, boozy peach frozen drinks, and other extras separate  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday, 5:00-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins and a limited number of Resy reservations reported by Eater; desserts and boozy peach frozen drinks available separately; tax/tip/service charge, takeout status, and any soda/sweet-tea add-on were not verified in checked sources.

2026-11-11 · Opens November 11; closes January 3, 2027 · theater · Al Hirschfeld Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 62.0  
Four illusionists perform a Broadway stage spectacle inspired by the Now You See Me films, with high-tech illusions, solo stunts, and large-scale magic.
